Method: ATOBench在运行时注入已登记的响应变换，并在相同环境中为每个变换后的回合配对原生回合；以首个受影响响应为对齐点，重建后续行动、证据恢复、停止决策与报告支撑关系。框架定义三类冻结观测契约：利用证明、资源所有权和可复用工件，并在450个回合上评估5条模型路线。

Result: 分析表明，更多的行动次数不必然代表更可靠的验证：智能体可能持续操作却已失去有效证据链。能够从欺骗响应中恢复的关键，在于重新发现可用证据，并在后续报告中完整保留和正确引用该证据。

Conclusion: ATOBench将欺骗性目标观测转化为可复现的过程级探针，使评测从单纯的最终结果扩展到行动、验证和报告之间的证据传递机制。该框架有助于识别自主渗透测试智能体在不可信观测条件下的验证鲁棒性与报告可信度。

Abstract: Autonomous penetration-testing agents rely on target responses. These responses guide both subsequent actions and the final report. A deceptive response can therefore redirect both the attack trajectory and the agent's verification process. However, final reports reveal little about how an agent interprets conflicting evidence, changes course, decides to stop, or turns observations into a vulnerability claim. We introduce ATOBench, an evaluation framework that makes this verification process observable. ATOBench injects registered response transformations at runtime and pairs each transformed episode with a native episode under the same environment. Each pair is aligned at the first affected response. A source-linked reconstruction then follows later actions, evidence recovery, stopping, and report support. Three frozen observation contracts cover exploit proof, resource ownership, and reusable artifacts. We evaluate five model routes over 450 episodes. The analysis shows that increased activity can mask a broken verification chain, while successful recovery depends on finding usable evidence and preserving it through reporting. ATOBench turns deceptive target observations into a reproducible probe of evidence handling in autonomous penetration testing. This process-level view extends offensive pentest agent evaluation beyond final outcomes by revealing how untrusted observations shape actions, verification, and reporting.

</details>


### [100] [OmniSphinx: Active Mix Networks (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13008)
*Daniel Schadt,Christoph Coijanovic,Shabi Shabani,Thorsten Strufe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出OmniSphinx，一种可编程的主动混合网络分组格式：发送方在分组中嵌入处理代码，使同一部署能够仿真多种既有混合网络格式，减少格式不兼容导致的软件与基础设施重复建设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合网络分组格式通常针对单一目标设计，彼此不兼容。支持不同格式往往需要分别部署软件和网络基础设施，造成运维与互操作成本上升。作者希望以统一格式承载多种处理语义。

Method: 设计OmniSphinx格式，将决定节点处理方式的代码嵌入发送方构造的分组中，从而形成主动混合网络。该机制旨在在单一网络部署中模拟其他分组格式；论文通过实证实验，将其仿真执行的计算时间和报头大小与原生执行进行比较。

Result: 针对典型使用场景，作者称OmniSphinx的仿真开销处于合理范围。以紧凑型Sphinx格式为例，计算时间约增加90微秒，报头大小增加33%。摘要未提供测试环境、吞吐量、延迟分布、安全性评估或其他格式的定量结果。

Conclusion: OmniSphinx以“分组携带处理逻辑”的统一抽象改善了多种混合网络格式之间的兼容性，并在Sphinx仿真中展示了可接受的额外成本。其实际适用性仍取决于嵌入代码的执行隔离、验证与资源约束机制，以及在更广泛负载和格式下的性能与安全表现。

Abstract: Mix networks are an important tool to implement anonymous communication, which protects not just the content but also the metadata of messages. Over time, various packet formats for mix networks have been proposed, usually with single, specific goals in mind. These formats are incompatible with each other, requiring separate software and infrastructure to be set up. In this paper, we propose a new format, OmniSphinx, which solves this issue. In OmniSphinx, senders embed code in their packets that determines how they must be processed. The resulting active mix network can emulate any other mix format within a single deployment. Our empirical evaluation shows that emulation in OmniSphinx incurs reasonable overhead compared to native execution for typical mix network use cases: For Sphinx, the most compact format, computation time increases by around 90μs, while headers increase by 33% in size.

</details>


### [101] [InterSAGE: The Secure and Verifiable Interoperability Protocol for An Internet of Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13030)
*Zhenhua Zou,Sheng Guo,Qiuyang Zhan,Lepeng Zhao,Shuo Li,Zhuotao Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出InterSAGE：一套面向“智能体互联网”的信任原生协议套件。它不替代既有通信协议，而是通过持久身份、能力发现、信任协商和责任追溯四层机制，为跨组织智能体协作提供可验证的安全基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体协议主要规范消息交换与工具调用，却缺少统一机制来证明智能体身份、授权范围、声明能力的真实性，以及任务委托后的责任归属。这使得跨组织环境中的发现、协作、权限转授和审计面临信任断裂问题。

Method: InterSAGE构建四层架构：持久身份层、发现层、信任协商层与责任追溯层。其核心原语包括：以身份卡绑定开发者、代码包、运营方和部署上下文；利用绑定去中心化标识符的可验证凭证清单进行能力感知发现；以单调能力衰减和双层访问控制实施信任协商；通过内核中介的密码学审计轨迹，将使用、委托及执行记录与智能体身份绑定，同时避免依赖共识账本。该方案被设计为与MCP、A2A、ANP和AG-UI等通信协议并行集成。

Result: 作者对超过50项相关工作进行比较，覆盖智能体协议、去中心化身份、OAuth/OIDC扩展、零信任治理、委托和审计架构。比较结论是，既有方案尚未将持久身份、能力感知发现、信任协商和责任追溯作为统一的四层信任底座共同实施。

Conclusion: InterSAGE的主要贡献在于提出可移植、显式且可验证的智能体信任语义，并将其与通信机制解耦，从而支持通信协议独立演进。摘要表明其定位为综合性架构与协议设计；其实际有效性仍需通过原型实现、互操作性测试、性能开销测量及安全攻击评估进一步验证。

Abstract: The emerging Internet of Agents enables LLM-powered agents to discover peers, invoke tools, and delegate tasks across organizational boundaries. Existing protocols increasingly define how agents exchange messages, but not how an agent proves its identity, authorization, advertised capabilities, or accountability after delegation. We present InterSAGE, a trust-native protocol suite that supplies this missing security substrate alongside, rather than in place of, communication protocols. InterSAGE comprises four layers: Persistent Identity, Discovery, Trust Negotiation, and Accountability. Its four core primitives are: (1) Agent Identity Cards that bind developer, code package, operator, and deployment context; (2) capability-aware discovery using DID-bound Verifiable Credential manifests; (3) trust negotiation combining monotonic capability attenuation with two-tier access control; and (4) kernel-mediated cryptographic audit trails that bind usage, delegation, and execution traces to agent identity without a consensus ledger. InterSAGE is designed to complement MCP, A2A, ANP, and AG-UI, allowing communication protocols to evolve independently while keeping trust semantics explicit, portable, and verifiable. We compare InterSAGE with more than 50 efforts spanning agent protocols, decentralized identity, OAuth/OIDC extensions, zero-trust governance, delegation, and audit architectures. We show that no prior architecture jointly enforces persistent identity, capability-aware discovery, trust negotiation, and accountability as a unified four-layer trust substrate for secure agent interoperability.

</details>


### [102] [Operationalizing Cyber Threat Intelligence with GraphRAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13050)
*Atul Kabra,Prakhar Paliwal,Manjesh K. Hanawal*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文比较知识图谱检索增强生成（Microsoft GraphRAG）与普通向量检索增强生成（Naive RAG）在从网络安全报告自动生成检测/狩猎计划中的表现，发现GraphRAG更能提取难以被快速替换的高层行为特征，生成的方案具有更强的持续有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化流程往往只从报告中提取IP地址、域名和文件哈希等易变指标，并将其转化为拦截列表。这类指标可被迅速轮换，导致检测规则很快失效。研究旨在验证：改变检索架构、引入知识图谱关系信息，是否能促使生成系统关注更稳定、更难规避的检测线索。

Method: 研究在控制变量条件下比较GraphRAG和Naive RAG：两者使用相同的输入报告、生成提示词和语言模型，仅替换检索模块。评估以Pyramid of Pain框架衡量检测计划所依赖线索的耐规避程度，并通过轮换报告中的IP、域名和文件哈希，测试检测计划的持续命中率。实验包括一个APT28报告的详细案例，以及来自四家供应商的九份真实威胁情报报告的重复比较。

Result: 在APT28案例中，轮换全部IP、域名和文件哈希后，GraphRAG生成计划仍保持100%的检测持续命中率，而Naive RAG仅为29%。在九份真实报告的扩展实验中，GraphRAG生成的计划总体上更稳定地覆盖Pyramid of Pain中更高、规避成本更大的层级。即使两种方案的总评分接近，GraphRAG在检测依据的耐久性方面仍表现更优。研究同时指出，生成提示词的措辞对结果影响显著，其重要性接近检索后端选择。

Conclusion: 知识图谱感知检索更适合作为自动生成可部署安全运营中心狩猎计划的基础架构，因为其可利用报告中的实体关系和行为上下文，减少对易失效指标的依赖。不过，结论主要建立在有限报告集与特定系统配置上；后续应在更多报告类型、实际部署环境、误报率和规则可执行性等维度进一步验证，并联合优化检索设计与提示词工程。

Abstract: When a security researcher publishes a report on a cyberattack, detection engineers are supposed to turn it into working detection rules. In practice, most automated attempts at this only extract the simplest clues from the report --- bad IP addresses, domain names, and file hashes --- and turn them into block lists. This is a weak strategy, because attackers can change these simple clues within hours or days, so the resulting detections stop working almost as soon as they are deployed. Security teams describe this idea with the Pyramid of Pain. This project asks whether feeding a report into a knowledge-graph retrieval system, Microsoft GraphRAG, rather than a standard vector-similarity retrieval system (Naive RAG), produces detection plans that rely more on these durable, top-of-pyramid clues. Both systems are given the same report, the same generation instructions, and the same language model to write the final plan; only the retrieval step differs. In a detailed case study of one APT28 report, the GraphRAG plan kept firing at 100\% of its detections after every IP address, domain, and file hash in the report was rotated, while the Naive RAG plan kept firing at only 29\%. Repeating the comparison across nine real CTI reports from four vendors confirms the same pattern: GraphRAG plans consistently reach higher, harder-to-evade levels of the pyramid, even when the two systems end up close on total score. The results support treating knowledge-graph-aware retrieval as the architecturally correct foundation for automatically generating SOC-deployable hunting plans, while showing that the wording of the generation prompt matters almost as much as the retrieval back-end itself.

</details>


### [103] [Smart Contract Invariants Protect Against Cybercriminals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13191)
*Sofia Bobadilla,Humaira Afrin,Angela Novelli,Martin Monperrus*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出并评估了以程序不变量保护智能合约的方案：在包含28个以太坊历史漏洞案例的INVARIANTEVAL基准上，人工编写的不变量均能阻止相应攻击，并通过PONDEREPLAY重放108,637笔历史交易验证其正确性与健全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约部署后难以修改，历史漏洞造成显著资产损失；现有防护技术的实际效果尚不明确。作者认为，能够约束合约状态与执行行为的程序不变量，可能为漏洞利用提供一种通用且强有力的防线。

Method: 构建实验流程：（1）建立INVARIANTEVAL基准，收录28个真实以太坊漏洞实例，并为每例配对一个可阻断漏洞利用的人工不变量；（2）使用PONDEREPLAY按历史顺序重放链上交易，检验不变量是否在正常历史执行中成立，并确认其能拒绝恶意利用交易；（3）进一步考察现有自动化工具发现此类不变量的能力，但摘要未给出该部分的具体工具、指标或结果。

Result: 所有28个基准案例中，配套不变量均可阻断相应漏洞利用。作者通过重放108,637笔历史交易验证了这些不变量在所覆盖历史执行中的正确性与健全性。摘要据此表明，不变量对该基准内的历史漏洞具有完整防护效果。

Conclusion: 程序不变量是智能合约运行时安全防护的有前景方法，且在所选真实漏洞基准上获得了强实证支持。不过，结论的适用范围受限于28个案例、人工编写不变量及历史交易覆盖；自动推导能力、部署开销、误报风险以及对未见漏洞类型的泛化性仍需依据全文实验进一步评估。

Abstract: Blockchains are among the most adversarial environments in computing. Billions are stolen by cybercriminals who exploit vulnerabilities. This is an open problem and no concept or technique has proven to really make a difference. In this paper, we claim that the classical notion of program invariant is perhaps the most powerful solution to the problem. We devise anoriginal experimental protocol to 1) study how invariants would have protected against past real-world attacks and 2) whether state-of-the-art automated tools can find them. The experimental toolchain is sophisticated. It is based on INVARIANTEVAL, a benchmark of 28 real Ethereum exploits, each paired with a human-authored invariant that blocks the attack. We validate every invariant with PONDEREPLAY, a replay framework that re-executes transactions in order to prove the correctness and soundness of smart contract invariants. We demonstrate that smart contract invariants block all the cybercriminal attacks in INVARIANTEVAL, fully validated by replaying 108,637 historical transactions. Our large-scale experiments clearly demonstrate that smart contract invariants protect against cybercriminals.

</details>


### [104] [A Commitment-Based Hybrid Post-Quantum Cryptographic Model for Multi-File Cloud Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13138)
*Lemdi Frank Prikutse,Regina Esi Turkson,Alimatu-Saadia Yussiff,Abdul-Lateef Yussiff,Maame G. Asante-Mensah*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出面向云存储批量上传的承诺式混合后量子认证模型：将整批密文经SHA3-256生成固定长度承诺，仅执行一次Ed25519与ML-DSA-65双签名，从而避免逐文件后量子签名带来的线性开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云存储客户端需要兼顾现有密码体系与后量子安全能力。直接将混合签名应用于多文件上传时，每个文件都需进行一次格密码签名，签名成本随文件数线性增长，并在大批量场景中成为认证延迟的主要瓶颈。

Method: 模型采用AES-256-GCM进行数据加密，采用X25519与ML-KEM-768混合密钥封装建立密钥保护，并采用Ed25519与ML-DSA-65混合双签名提供认证。对批次内全部密文计算SHA3-256批量承诺，将所有密文绑定到单个固定长度摘要，再对该摘要进行一次双签名；因此每批ML-DSA签名次数由n次降为1次。

Result: 在商用客户端平台上进行20次重复实验的平均结果表明，所提方案的签名阶段耗时随批量规模增长基本保持稳定，而逐文件双签名基线呈线性增长。在n=1000时，相对于使用相同其余密码组件的逐文件双签名基线，100 KB、1 MB和10 MB文件的签名阶段加速比分别为629倍、606倍和725倍。

Conclusion: 批量承诺将后量子签名的高成本从逐文件操作转化为逐批操作，在不改变对整批密文绑定能力的前提下显著改善大规模上传认证性能。该设计尤其适合高批量云存储客户端，但摘要未说明批次成员证明、失败恢复、动态增删文件及端到端安全形式化证明等部署细节。

Abstract: Cloud storage clients increasingly require authentication that remains secure against future quantum-capable adversaries, motivating hybrid constructions that combine classical primitives with standardized post-quantum alternatives. Extended naively to multi-file upload, such constructions incur a per-file lattice signing cost that dominates authentication time and becomes prohibitive at realistic batch sizes. This paper presents a commitment-based hybrid post-quantum model that addresses this bottleneck. It comprises AES-256-GCM bulk encryption, a hybrid X25519 with ML-KEM-768 key encapsulation mechanism, and a hybrid Ed25519 with ML-DSA-65 dual signature, computed over a SHA3-256 batch commitment. The commitment binds all ciphertexts in a batch to a single fixed-size digest that is signed once, reducing the number of post-quantum signature invocations per batch from n to one, independent of batch size; the remaining encryption and hashing is bounded by fast symmetric throughput. On a commodity client platform, averaged over 20 repetitions, this holds signing-phase time near-constant as the batch grows while the per-file baseline scales linearly. At n = 1000, the model reduces signing-phase time by factors of 629, 606, and 725 for 100 KB, 1 MB, and 10 MB files respectively, against a per-file dual-signing baseline sharing every other primitive.

</details>


### [105] [Slow and Steady: Preventing MEV with Verifiable Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13271)
*Zeta Avarikioti,Dimitris Karakostas,Karl Kreder,Shreekara Shastry*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于可验证延迟的MEV防御机制：交易生成需经历可验证的时间延迟，使区块创建者难以及时针对新出现的MEV机会插入或调整交易，同时维持系统活性。作者在拜占庭参与者模型和理性参与者博弈模型下给出正向安全结论，并说明该方法的理论边界与工程可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MEV机会允许区块创建者或相关参与者通过重排、插入或延迟交易获利，可能损害交易公平性与用户利益。现有防护往往依赖特定排序规则、可信组件或复杂市场设计；本文试图通过限制参与者对机会出现后的即时反应能力，提供更基础的密码学防御路径。

Method: 核心方法是在交易生成过程中强制施加可公开验证的延迟。若区块创建者在MEV机会出现后才试图快速构造相应交易，其行为将无法满足延迟要求，或会导致协议活性受损。论文分别在存在任意偏离行为的拜占庭模型、以及参与者以收益最大化为目标的博弈论模型中分析机制效果；同时推导负向界限以刻画无法防御的情形，并讨论可验证延迟函数等现实实现细节，结合历史MEV数据评估覆盖能力。

Result: 摘要声称该机制在两类理论模型中均取得正向结果，表明其能够抑制一部分依赖快速反应的MEV策略；负向结果进一步限定了防御能力的适用范围。基于历史MEV数据的实证式评估显示，该机制在现实参数下有望阻止现有多数MEV威胁。摘要未提供具体安全定义、延迟参数、数据集范围、量化比例或性能开销，因此无法据此判断实际收益与成本的精确规模。

Conclusion: 该工作将MEV防御转化为“反应时间约束”问题，并以可验证延迟提供可审计的执行手段，兼顾理论安全分析和工程落地讨论。其主要价值在于对拜占庭和理性行为均给出论证，同时明确方法边界。后续评估应重点检查延迟机制的部署成本、对正常交易确认时延与吞吐量的影响、激励兼容性，以及对预先预测或长期布局型MEV策略的防护不足。

Abstract: Our work presents a defense mechanism against Maximal Extractable Value (MEV) opportunities in distributed ledgers. The mechanism relies on the idea of enforcing a verifiable delay when generating transactions, such that a block creator cannot react to the appearance of a MEV opportunity without breaking liveness. We present positive results both in the Byzantine setting and in a game theoretic model of rational participants. We additionally present negative bounds that outline the limitations of this line of defense. Finally, we explore real-world implementation details of verifiable delays and show that, based on historical MEV data, our mechanism could realistically help prevent most existing MEV threats.

</details>


### [106] [VR-Themis: A Scalable Framework for Virtual Reality Application Clone Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13290)
*Gengyang Xu,Hanyang Guo,Hong-Ning Dai,Weizhi Meng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出面向虚拟现实应用的两阶段克隆检测框架VR-Themis，通过层级—对象—行为（HOB）特征兼顾大规模筛查与细粒度判别，在4,277个应用中识别出307个疑似克隆应用且未出现误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有应用克隆检测方法主要针对传统移动平台，难以表征虚拟现实应用在场景层级、交互对象及行为逻辑方面的特征，因此对虚拟现实应用克隆的检测效果不足。

Method: VR-Themis采用两阶段流程：第一阶段提取可获取的统计特征，对全部应用进行粗粒度聚类，以缩小候选范围并提升大规模数据处理能力；第二阶段针对可疑候选应用，依据定义的HOB度量计算相似性，开展层级、对象与行为维度的深入比对。

Result: 在收集的4,277个虚拟现实应用上，框架检测到307个疑似克隆应用；摘要报告该实验中未观察到误报，表明方法具有较高的检测精度与可扩展性。

Conclusion: VR-Themis将虚拟现实应用的结构与交互行为特征纳入克隆检测，弥补了传统移动应用检测方法的适配不足。其两阶段设计有效平衡了计算效率和分析深度，但摘要未说明召回率、标注验证流程及跨应用商店泛化能力，这些仍需通过完整论文进一步评估。

Abstract: Repackaging of mobile applications (aka app cloning) not only threatens the security and privacy of mobile users but also infringes upon the copyright of the original app developers. However, existing detection methods that primarily focus on mobile platforms (such as Android) fail to capture the essential features of virtual reality (VR). Consequently, they are inadequate for effectively detecting cloned VR apps, which have often been targeted by illegal users in the VR market. Considering the unique features of VR apps, this paper proposes a two-stage app clone detection framework, namely VR-Themis, based on \emph{Hierarchy-Object-Behaviour} (HOB). Firstly, VR-Themis exploits the coarse-grained stage to cluster apps based on their retrievable statistical features, making this tool scalable to large-scale VR app datasets. Then, in the fine-grained stage, VR-Themis performs in-depth analysis of the suspicious apps (identified in the first stage) by calculating similarity using our defined \emph{HOB metrics}. Our extensive experiments indicate that VR-Themis successfully detects 307 suspected clone apps from the collected 4,277 VR apps without false positives, demonstrating its effectiveness and scalability.

</details>


### [107] [TeleGapper: On the (un)reliability of Privacy Policies in Telegram Mini apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13390)
*Luca Ferrari,Mariano Ceccato,Luca Verderame*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出TeleGapper黑盒动态分析框架，评估Telegram Mini Apps的运行时数据流与隐私披露的一致性。对278个可用应用的测量显示，该生态存在显著的第三方数据传输披露不足与用户控制缺失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Telegram Mini Apps在WebView中运行，既可访问平台上下文，又具备标准Web能力和不受限的外部网络通信能力。相较于受严格执行框架约束的小程序生态，其数据可经普通Web请求发送至分析、广告或追踪服务，但相关隐私实践此前缺乏系统研究。尤其是平台默认隐私政策可能无法准确反映单个应用的实际行为。

Method: 作者构建TeleGapper，对Mini Apps进行黑盒动态分析：捕获运行时网络流量，识别第三方通信，并将实际观测到的数据流与应用声明的隐私信息进行比对。实验样本为从tApps Center收集的278个可正常运行的Mini Apps。

Result: 59.4%的应用至少连接了一个未在隐私披露中说明的第三方；78.8%的应用仅依赖Telegram提供的默认隐私政策；全部样本均未提供用户同意或退出机制。结果表明，默认化、泛化的隐私声明与实际网络通信行为之间存在普遍不一致。

Conclusion: Telegram Mini Apps生态存在明显的隐私透明度与合规性缺口。TeleGapper证明，基于运行时流量的黑盒审计可有效发现披露遗漏；平台与开发者应提供更具应用特异性的隐私声明，并建立可验证的数据实践披露及用户同意、拒绝或退出控制机制。

Abstract: Telegram Mini Apps are Web applications embedded within the Telegram client, forming an ecosystem of third-party services within one of the world's most widely used messaging platforms. Despite their growing adoption and access to Telegram-provided context, their privacy properties remain largely unexplored. Unlike ecosystems such as WeChat, which rely on tightly controlled, proprietary execution frameworks, Telegram adopts a different model: Mini Apps run inside a WebView, combining platform-provided context with standard Web capabilities and unrestricted outbound networking. This enables applications to transmit sensitive information to analytics, advertising, tracking, or other third parties through ordinary Web requests, often with limited visibility.
  Privacy disclosures are therefore critical for transparency. Telegram allows Mini Apps either to define an application-specific privacy policy or to rely on a platform-provided default policy. While the latter reduces the developer's disclosure burden, it may lead to generic statements that do not accurately capture actual data practices of individual Mini Apps.
  In this paper, we present TeleGapper, a black-box dynamic analysis framework to assess the privacy posture of Mini Apps by capturing runtime network traffic, identifying third-party communications, and comparing observed data flows against disclosed privacy information. We evaluate 278 working Mini Apps collected from tApps Center, a community-driven catalogue for discovering third-party applications in Telegram. We find that 59.4% contact at least one undisclosed third party, 78.8% rely exclusively on Telegram's default privacy policy, and none provides a consent or opt-out mechanism. These findings expose a substantial transparency and compliance gap in a widely used yet understudied ecosystem.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [A Rig of Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12409)
*Emma Tye*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出以交换半环（commutative rig）刻画有限代数数据类型，并将半环等式对应为数据类型间的同构；同时以部分同构表达类型嵌入到更大表示类型的过程，例如位填充。该框架旨在让高级函数式语言中的程序级数据建模与底层二进制布局需求能够系统衔接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高级函数式语言通常弱化开发者对数据运行时表示的控制，但实际系统常需因性能、存储效率、互操作或既有二进制格式而定制布局。程序中的抽象数据结构与期望的二进制表示并不必然一致，因此需要一种既正确、又高效且可用性良好的形式化机制来描述布局及其转换。

Method: 将有限代数数据类型映射为交换半环：和类型对应加法，积类型对应乘法，有限类型的基数可由相应半环表达式描述。利用半环中的等式构造数据类型之间的双向同构，从而将代数化简、分配律、结合律等结构变换解释为可验证的表示转换。对于无法完全双向恢复的信息扩展，如向位级表示中加入填充位，则用部分同构描述：编码方向可嵌入更大的类型，而解码方向仅在满足有效性条件的值上定义。

Result: 摘要声称该模型能够统一表达两类关键操作：其一是保持信息完整的数据类型重构，即由交换半环等式导出的类型同构；其二是带表示冗余或有效性约束的布局扩展，即由部分同构描述的嵌入。该方法因而为从高层代数数据类型推导、验证底层布局转换提供了数学语义。摘要未提供实现细节、性能实验或具体语言集成结果。

Conclusion: 该工作的核心贡献是以交换半环及其等式为有限代数数据类型布局变换提供代数基础，并以部分同构补足严格同构无法覆盖的填充和受限表示场景。其潜在价值在于使二进制布局优化和遗留格式适配能够在保持类型结构与转换正确性的前提下进行；实际效果仍需结合具体编译器实现、自动化程度和运行时开销评估。

Abstract: In high-level functional languages, the compiler often gives users little control over the runtime representation of data types. Yet how we model data structures at the program level can be different to how we want to represent them at the binary level, for efficiency or legacy reasons. Hence being able to describe data layouts and their transformations for data types is a useful and necessary part of programming, but difficult to do correctly, efficiently and ergonomically. We present a model of finite algebraic data types as a commutative rig (a ring without additive inverses), where the rig-equalities are modelled by isomorphisms between data. Using this approach, we can also model embedding a data type into a larger type (e.g. bit-padding) as a partial isomorphism.

</details>


### [109] [Stream-based Online and Offline Monitoring under Measurement Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13211)
*Bernd Finkbeiner,Martin Fränzle,Florian Kohn,Paul Kröger*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出RLola：对流式运行时监测语言Lola的鲁棒扩展。该方法用符号化松弛变量建模传感器测量噪声，提供在线与离线监测算法，并给出可保证常数内存在线执行的语言片段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统流式监测通常将传感器输入视为精确真值，但实际测量含有噪声与误差，且误差会在后续计算和聚合中累积或放大。直接采用区间算术又可能产生别名问题，导致结果过度保守或不精确，因此需要能够显式、可靠传播不确定性的监测机制。

Method: RLola在Lola规范中引入松弛变量，以符号方式表达测量误差并避免区间算术的变量依赖/别名缺陷。论文设计两类算法：在线算法持续处理实时输入并反馈断言状态；基于SMT的离线算法对完整轨迹求解，判断是否存在满足全部断言的假设真实轨迹。同时，作者识别出一个可自动编译为常数内存在线监视器的丰富RLola子片段，并将实现集成至RTLola框架。

Result: 在线监测能够对运行中系统提供实时断言状态，但在不确定性条件下可能遗漏部分违例；离线SMT分析可利用完整轨迹检验是否存在满足条件的真实轨迹，因此能发现在线方法未识别的问题。对于所定义的受限片段，系统可保证在线监测的常数内存消耗。实验通过综合示例评估了实现的精度与运行时间。

Conclusion: RLola将测量不确定性纳入流式运行时验证的语义与算法框架，在实时性、精度和资源约束之间提供了分层方案：常数内存在线监测适合部署场景，SMT离线分析适合更强的事后诊断。其核心贡献是以松弛变量实现比朴素区间传播更稳健的不确定性处理。

Abstract: Stream-based monitoring is a runtime verification approach for cyber-physical systems that translates streams of input data, such as sensor readings, into streams of aggregate statistics and verdicts about the safety of the system. It is usually assumed that the values on the input streams represent fully accurate measurements of the physical world. In reality, however, physical sensors are prone to measurement noise and errors. These errors are further amplified by the processing and aggregation steps within the monitor. This paper introduces RLola, a robust extension of the stream-based specification language Lola. RLola incorporates the concept of slack variables, which symbolically represent measurement noise while avoiding the aliasing problem of interval arithmetic.
  We present algorithms for both online and offline monitoring of RLola specifications. Since monitoring RLola specifications may require unbounded memory in general, we identify a rich fragment of RLola that can be automatically translated into monitors with guaranteed constant memory usage for online monitoring. An online RLola monitor observes a live system and provides real-time feedback on the current status of specified assertions. A satisfiability-modulo-theories-based offline algorithm analyzes complete system traces and determines whether a hypothetical ground-truth trace exists that satisfies all assertions at all time points. The offline algorithm can therefore detect violations that the online algorithm may miss. We implement these algorithms in the existing RTLola framework and evaluate their precision and running time based on a comprehensive example.

</details>


### [110] [Multiobjective Preexpectation Reasoning for Probabilistic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13268)
*Lena Verscht,Hannah Mertens,Kevin Batz,Sebastian Junges,Benjamin Lucien Kaminski,Joost-Pieter Katoen*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出面向含非确定性的概率程序的多目标优化验证与策略综合框架：通过多目标前期望变换器，在程序语义层面刻画帕累托前沿上的可达收益，并综合可见证目标达成的随机化策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率程序分析通常聚焦单一目标，难以直接处理多个期望收益之间的权衡；而基于显式状态空间的多目标马尔可夫决策过程分析对无限状态系统不适用或代价高。论文旨在提供一种无需有限状态假设、可在程序层次进行符号化推理的多目标策略综合方法。

Method: 作者定义多目标前期望变换器：输入为后期望向量，输出为可同时实现数值的集合，该集合以凸Hoare幂域元素表示。该语义保守扩展最弱前期望，并将标准循环推理规则提升到多目标情形。在此基础上，论文给出策略综合规则，将见证策略构造成“混合确定化”：对多个非概率确定化程序按概率随机选择。最后，将变换器语义及综合规则与操作性MDP语义对照，证明其健全性。

Result: 该框架能够在不要求状态空间有限的前提下，推导多目标期望收益的同时可达区域，并为满足给定权衡点的解生成随机化策略。理论上，所定义的语义和策略综合规则均相对于MDP操作语义健全；实践上，作者通过多个案例展示了该机制的适用性。

Conclusion: 论文将多目标帕累托优化、概率程序演算与策略综合统一到程序级推理框架中，为无限状态MDP的多目标优化提供了符号化替代路径。其关键贡献是以凸集合语义准确表达多目标可达值，并以随机混合确定化策略实现相应的可行权衡。

Abstract: Probabilistic programs with nondeterminism model planning problems in which a strategy resolves the nondeterminism to optimize an expected outcome. We study the multiobjective setting, optimizing several outcomes at once along a Pareto front, and provide a deductive, program-level account of strategy synthesis. Its core is a multiobjective preexpectation transformer mapping a tuple of postexpectations to the set of simultaneously achievable values, an element of the convex Hoare powerdomain. It conservatively extends weakest preexpectations and lifts standard loop rules. We develop rules to synthesize witnessing strategies as mixed determinizations that randomize over non-probabilistic determinizations. We prove the transformer and synthesis rules sound against an operational MDP semantics, without requiring a finite state space: our approach can be seen as a symbolic approach - at program level - for multiobjective optimization over infinite MDPs. We demonstrate our machinery using various case studies.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [SynWeaver: Website-Prior Task and Trajectory Co-Synthesis for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12429)
*Ruitao Wang,Yuwen Hao,Menglin Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出SynWeaver，一种利用网站先验进行任务—轨迹协同合成的框架。其通过构建网站地图、学习页面与状态转移先验、联合修正任务和执行轨迹，生成可执行且语义一致的监督数据，并在WebArena和WebVoyager上优于现有合成基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网页智能体在未见网站上的泛化能力受限于缺乏网站特定监督。现有基于探索的数据合成方法通常存在两项不足：对网站功能覆盖不完整，以及缺少网站先验导致生成与实际能力不符的任务。这会降低轨迹数据的多样性、有效性和后续训练收益。

Method: SynWeaver包含三个阶段：第一，结构化探索目标网站，建立覆盖不同功能页面状态及可执行交互的网站地图；第二，从地图中提取页面级和状态转移级监督，训练具备UI感知能力的网站特定先验模型，以生成更贴合网站实际功能的任务；第三，执行任务—轨迹协同合成，在任务描述与执行过程不一致时共同更新二者，并通过验证与修复机制筛选出可执行、语义对齐的监督样本。

Result: 在WebArena和WebVoyager实验中，SynWeaver相较强数据合成基线表现更优。其生成的监督数据能够提升网页智能体在目标网站内的学习效果，同时改善向未见网站迁移时的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 网站地图提供的显式功能覆盖和网站先验，是提高网页任务合成真实性与覆盖度的关键；任务与轨迹的协同更新及后验验证修复，则能进一步保证样本可执行性和语义一致性。该框架为低人工标注成本下构建高质量网页智能体训练数据提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Web agents often struggle to generalize to unseen websites because they lack website-specific supervision. Recent exploration-based data synthesis methods reduce manual annotation, but they still face two key limitations: they often fail to cover the full functionality of a website, and without sufficient website prior knowledge, they tend to propose hallucinated tasks, which in turn limits the diversity and efficiency of downstream trajectory synthesis. We present \textbf{SynWeaver}, a website-prior task-trajectory co-synthesis framework designed to address these challenges. SynWeaver first performs structured website exploration and constructs a website map that covers a broad set of functionally distinct page states and executable interactions on the target website. It then derives page-level and transition-level supervision from this map to train a UI-aware model with website-specific priors, enabling more grounded task proposals. Finally, SynWeaver performs collaborative task-trajectory synthesis, jointly updating the task and execution trajectory when they become inconsistent, and then verifies and repairs the collected results to produce executable, semantically aligned supervision. Experiments on WebArena and WebVoyager demonstrate that SynWeaver consistently outperforms strong synthesis baselines and yields more effective supervision for both in-domain and out-of-domain generalization.

</details>


### [112] [Specification-first convergence with an AI coding agent: a case study of dismantling a core architectural invariant across 189 files in a 717k-line codebase with no test oracle and no human code review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12440)
*Joel Abenhaim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文报告了一项由AI编程代理完成的大规模架构重构个案：在无人工代码审查、无既有行为预言机的条件下，代理通过“规格优先—多轮审计—原子实现—编译/测试反馈—冻结规格验证”流程，修改大型TypeScript生产系统的核心生命周期约束，并声称实现了流式生成在界面关闭后持续运行及重新打开后无丢失、无重复地重连。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究关注传统渐进式重构难以处理的跨模块核心不变量拆除问题。目标是检验AI代理能否在超大规模、强耦合代码库中，以可审计的规格驱动过程替代依赖人工持续监督的重构方式，并降低大型架构变更的时间与成本。

Method: 对象为约71.8万行、3,648个文件的TypeScript应用。代理先生成形式化规格，并用14轮迭代将规格与源代码对照审计；随后进行一次原子化实现，通过编译与测试循环修正；最后以冻结后的规格为基准开展17轮代码验证。31轮审计共识别并修复201项缺陷，以连续两轮验证均无发现作为经验性收敛标准。研究还公开了规格与会话日志，支持过程复查及语言模型一致性检查。

Result: 变更直接涉及189个文件，其中新增31个；连同抽取阶段的提交，共涉及288个文件、34,770行新增与16,422行删除。作者报告该改动在首次及后续约30次会话中均符合目标行为，未观察到缺陷；完成周期为3天，成本为2,430美元。核心功能结果是：流式生成不再依赖面板持续打开，面板重新打开时可连接回同一仍在运行的流。

Conclusion: 该个案表明，规格优先与重复审计结合的AI代理流程，可能具备完成传统上接近重写级别架构变更的能力，并能产出较强的过程可追溯性。但证据仅来自单一案例，成功判定主要依赖作者观察、编译测试和规格一致性审计，且明确缺少独立行为预言机与人工代码审查；因此尚不能推断其对不同系统、不同任务或长期可靠性的普适效果。后续应采用独立复现、外部评审、故障注入、长期运行观测及与人工团队和其他代理流程的对照实验验证。

Abstract: This paper reports a single, fully instrumented case study of a large-scale architectural refactoring by an AI coding agent under a specification-first protocol, with no human review of the generated code and no pre-existing oracle to validate the target behaviour. The task, dismantling a central invariant across a large interdependent codebase, was assessed by the author as effectively infeasible through incremental refactoring, the kind of change that conventionally calls for a rewrite instead. Under the protocol described here, the agent completed it successfully.
  The system is a 717,725-line production TypeScript application across 3,648 files. The task required dismantling a core lifetime invariant: the guarantee that a UI panel remains open for the duration of an AI request. The target behaviour was that a streaming generation survives the closing of its panel and can be reattached, on reopening, to the same live stream with no loss or duplication.
  The protocol: formal specification by the agent, 14 refinement cycles auditing that specification against the source code, atomic implementation, a compile/test feedback loop, then 17 verification cycles auditing the code against the frozen specification. Across 31 audit passes, 201 defects were corrected before any human executed the program. The convergence criterion was empirical: two consecutive verification passes returning zero findings.
  The change touched 189 files (31 new); with the extraction phase, the two commits total 288 files, 34,770 insertions, 16,422 deletions. Across the first and roughly thirty later sessions, the software behaved as specified, no bug observed. Elapsed: three days; cost: USD 2,430.
  The full specification and raw session logs, 1,500+ pages in French, are published as evidence, allowing inspection of the process and submission to a language model for consistency checking.

</details>


### [113] [Does It Render Everywhere? A Study of Cross-Environment Compatibility in MLLM-Generated Webpages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12518)
*Ziyun Guo,Jingyu Xiao,Yuqiang Sun,Yintong Huo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文系统研究由多模态大语言模型生成的网页在不同浏览器与设备环境中的渲染兼容性，并提出数据集WebCompat和离线检测器XCompat。结果显示，跨环境兼容性问题普遍存在：68%的生成网页至少出现一种问题；XCompat在测试集上取得0.903的F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网页生成评测通常只在固定浏览器和设备配置下衡量视觉保真度，无法反映真实部署中的跨环境渲染稳定性。论文旨在填补这一评测空白，识别AI生成前端代码的常见兼容性症状及其代码层根因。

Method: 作者构建WebCompat数据集，包含2,032个标注实例：由8种代表性AI工具生成的网页，分别在9种浏览器—设备组合中渲染。研究对兼容性问题的发生率、用户可感知症状和代码级根因进行系统分析；进一步结合网页截图的视觉信息与DOM树的结构信息，开发轻量级离线检测器XCompat。

Result: 68%的AI生成网页存在至少一种跨环境兼容性问题。最主要的问题是影响整页布局的缩放与响应式失效，占88.3%，典型表现为页面直接压缩以适配屏幕、字体过小，或缩放不匹配导致内容被截断。局部元素问题，如图像变形或组件缺失，占13.4%。尽管多数模型会生成响应式设计相关代码，但往往未能正确实现。XCompat在WebCompat-test上获得0.903的F1分数，优于既有兼容性检测工具及LLM基线。

Conclusion: 仅依据单一环境下的视觉相似度评估AI网页生成质量是不充分的，跨浏览器和跨设备兼容性应成为关键可靠性指标。WebCompat提供了可复现的评测基础，XCompat则表明融合截图与DOM结构能够有效识别兼容性缺陷；未来前端代码生成系统需要更重视响应式规则的正确实现与多环境验证。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have been increasingly adopted to automate webpage generation from visual designs (e.g., screenshots). However, existing evaluations are limited to visual fidelity assessment under a fixed browser-device configuration. Such a setting overlooks the cross-environment rendering compatibility for real-world deployments.
  To address this gap, we present the first systematic empirical study of cross-environment compatibility in AI-generated webpages. Specifically, we construct WebCompat, a dataset of 2,032 annotated instances, comprising webpages generated by 8 representative AI tools, each rendered across 9 browser-and-device combinations. We analyze the prevalence of compatibility issues, their user-perceptible symptoms, and underlying code-level root causes. Our findings reveal that 68% of generated webpages exhibit at least one compatibility issue, underscoring the pervasive reliability concerns surrounding MLLM-generated front-end artifacts. The most prevalent symptoms are failures that disrupt the entire page layout (88.3%): pages shrink directly to fit the target screen with too small fonts, or exhibit scale mismatches that produce cut-off content. Failures localized to individual elements, such as image distortion or missing components, are comparatively less common (13.4%). Furthermore, although most MLLMs incorporate responsive design patterns into the generation, they fail to properly implement these codes.
  Guided by the findings, we develop XCompat, a lightweight offline compatibility issue detector that combines visual screenshots and the structural DOM tree for analysis. It achieves an F1 score of 0.903 on the WebCompat-test, outperforming the existing compatibility checking tools and LLM baselines. All datasets and tools are released to support future research on rendering reliability in MLLM-based front-end code generation.

</details>


### [114] [Memorization Diagnostics for Code LLMs Should be Scale-Aware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12771)
*Prateek Kumar Rajput,Abdoul Aziz Bonkoungou,Alberick Euraste Djiré,Xunzhu Tang,Yewei Song,Iyiola Emmanuel Olatunji,El Hacen Diallo,Jacques Klein,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文指出，现有用于检测代码大语言模型“记忆”行为的探针在模型规模增大后显著失效。作者认为，探针实际混淆了“表征负载”与“记忆”两个因素；在剥离二者后，大模型对输入表面形式的鲁棒适应能力比是否复现训练样本更具实际意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码模型的高分可能来自训练数据污染后的记忆，也可能来自可泛化的理解能力。既有研究常用同义替换、死代码插入和基于对数概率的扰动测试来判定记忆，但这些方法在大规模模型、甚至已知受污染基准上都难以发挥作用。论文旨在解释这种规模相关的探针失效，并重新界定记忆评估应测量的对象。

Method: 作者比较了编码器式扰动探针与解码器式对数概率探针在不同规模模型上的表现，观察其随规模扩展而衰退的现象。随后在数值问题上施加可逆数学变换，通过保持解空间等价但改变输入表征形式的方式，将模型处理表面表达变化的表征负载与对具体样本或答案的记忆相分离。

Result: 传统扰动会明显影响较小模型，却难以撼动较大模型，因此不能稳定作为规模化模型记忆程度的证据。可逆变换实验表明，扩展后的编码器能够承受较高的表征负载，并仍收敛到正确的解族。由此，模型在形式变化下保持功能正确性的能力，不能被简单归因为训练样本记忆，也不应被现有探针误判。

Conclusion: 论文主张未来的记忆评估必须显式分离表征负载和记忆，而非依赖将两者隐性耦合的扰动或概率测试。对于软件工程应用，更关键的评价目标是模型能否在不同表面形式下生成可用且正确的解；即使训练记忆会抬高受污染基准成绩，其实际重要性也应在控制表征负载后重新判断。

Abstract: The extent to which large language models for code rely on memorization over genuine understanding remains highly debated. While current literature frequently reports widespread memorization, evaluating the underlying probing techniques across dense architectures reveals a severe breakdown in their utility at scale. Traditional encoder-style probes using perturbations such as synonym fuzzing or dead-code insertion struggle to expose memorization in scaled models, even on known-contaminated benchmarks, and decoder-style probes that rely on log probabilities show similar performance degradation. The specific mode of failure for these probes, particularly why such techniques disrupt smaller models but fail to impact larger ones, motivates us to untangle representation load from memorization rather than treating them as a single phenomenon. By applying invertible mathematical transforms to numeric problems, we isolate these two factors and reveal that scaled encoders successfully absorb substantial representation load while still converging on the correct family of solutions. In practical software engineering, this ability to adapt to varying surface forms is what truly matters for usability and generalizability in LLM and agentic applications. Whether a specific solution was seen during training becomes a much less pressing question because although memorization inflates scores on contaminated benchmarks, factoring out representation load makes it debatable how much we should truly care if a functional answer was originally memorized. Future evaluations must therefore be built around separating these phenomena rather than relying on methodologies that quietly entangle them.

</details>


### [115] [Dissecting Software Graphs: Structural Insights for Driver-Guided Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12859)
*Baihong Chen,Hua Ming,Weifeng Pan,Tian Xie,Haipeng Cai,Wen Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文研究多执行模式软件中的多驱动模糊测试，提出以静态调用图为共享骨架、将各驱动动态覆盖投影为驱动诱导子图的结构分析框架。实验表明，在相同总预算下，多驱动策略显著优于最佳单驱动基线，但不同驱动的贡献高度不均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模糊测试评估主要采用覆盖率与缺陷数量指标，难以解释命令行选项、子命令和配置标志所形成的不同执行模式，如何在程序结构上互补、重叠或遗漏。因此，作者希望从软件结构视角刻画多驱动测试的有效性及其剩余探索空间。

Method: 方法包含四个阶段：构建静态调用图骨架；对不同驱动配置执行模糊测试并采集动态覆盖；将覆盖信息映射到调用图以生成驱动诱导子图；围绕研究问题分析子图的凝聚性、碎片化程度、模块性、重叠关系及未充分探索区域。评估对象为27个OSS-Fuzz来源的C/C++项目，共43个可执行文件和854种驱动配置。

Result: 在相同总资源预算下，多驱动模糊测试使调用图节点覆盖提高27.9%，控制流图边覆盖提高73.5%；并发现11个单驱动方法大多未能触及的独有缺陷与异常行为。分析还发现，各驱动的边际贡献差异明显；诱导子图在凝聚性、碎片化和模块性方面存在显著差异；剩余未覆盖结构呈现若干重复出现的模式，而非均质的长尾区域。

Conclusion: 多驱动模糊测试不应仅被视为增加输入或配置数量的问题，其本质是对程序结构进行协同探索的问题。调用图骨架与驱动诱导子图能够揭示驱动间的互补性和冗余性，并可为驱动选择、资源分配及后续覆盖引导策略提供依据。

Abstract: Many software systems expose multiple execution modes through command-line options, subcommands, and configuration flags. For such programs, fuzzing depends on both mutated inputs and the invoked mode. Yet evaluations still focus on coverage and bug counts, leaving unclear how execution modes partition, overlap, and miss software structure, and how these differences affect effectiveness. We present an empirical study of software structure under multi-driver fuzzing. We propose a structural abstraction that uses a static call graph as a shared backbone and projects driver-specific dynamic coverage onto it to derive driver-induced subgraphs. Based on this abstraction, we develop a four-phase methodology for backbone construction, fuzzing and profiling, graph-based analysis, and research-question-driven evaluation. We apply it to 27 OSS-Fuzz-derived C/C++ projects, spanning 43 executables and 854 driver configurations. Under the same total budget, multi-driver fuzzing outperforms the best single-driver baseline, increasing covered call-graph nodes by 27.9% and CFG-edge coverage by 73.5%, and revealing 11 unique bugs and abnormal behaviors largely missed by single-driver fuzzing. However, driver contributions are uneven, subgraphs differ substantially in cohesion, fragmentation, modularity, overlap, and residual under-exploration follows recurring regimes rather than a homogeneous tail. These results show that multi-driver fuzzing is fundamentally a structural exploration problem.

</details>


### [116] [Requirements-Augmented Generation for Trustworthy Acceptance Testing of LLM-Based Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12970)
*Fanyu Wang,Chetan Arora,Zhenping Xie,Yonghui Liu,Kla Tantithamthavorn,Aldeida Aleti,Siwei Jiang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出面向大语言模型软件的自动化验收测试框架：以需求增强生成构造上下文感知测试预言机，并以置信度校准的级联判决控制结论可靠性。工业案例显示，该框架在预言机质量、判决准确率和成本效率方面均具备实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型软件的输出具有随机性和强上下文依赖，同一输入在不同用户画像与软件状态下可能对应不同的合理回答。因此，传统依赖确定性预期输出的验收测试和测试预言机难以适用。核心挑战是自动理解用户意图、整合动态需求约束，并对生成预言机及测试结论的不确定性进行可靠管理。

Method: 方法包含两部分。其一，需求增强生成（REAG）通过自适应检索增强生成检索软件需求、领域知识和用户画像，并结合自推理生成与上下文匹配的测试预言机。其二，置信度校准级联判决通过模拟专家一致性量化判决可靠度：高置信度样本直接接受，模糊样本升级处理，低置信度样本拒绝判定；同时采用保序风险控制为可靠性提供经验性保障。

Result: 在生产级营养咨询应用的案例中，REAG生成预言机的平均质量评分为3.91/5，82%的样本达到合格或边际合格水平。置信度校准级联机制取得98.8%的判决准确率，并通过过滤低质量输出将预言机质量提升至4.30/5；相较单一判决器基线，成本效率提升31.7%。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，将需求检索、意图推断与可靠性校准结合，可有效缓解大语言模型软件验收测试中预言机缺失和结论不稳定的问题。REAG提升了预言机的情境适配性，级联判决则在准确性、质量与成本之间实现更稳健的权衡，具备工业部署潜力。

Abstract: LLM-based software (LBS) integrates large language models as core components to deliver flexible, personalised responses. Unlike traditional software with deterministic outputs, LBSs exhibit context-dependent, stochastic behaviour that renders classical acceptance testing and test oracles insufficient: the same query may require fundamentally different responses depending on user personas and software context. This gap creates an urgent need for automated acceptance testing frameworks that autonomously interpret user instructions, while reliably inferring user intentions in a changing environment. In this paper, we present an automated acceptance testing framework for LBS with calibrated verdict reliability via two technical contributions. First, we introduce Requirements-Augmented Generation (REAG), which interprets user intentions by retrieving relevant software requirements, domain knowledge, and personas via adaptive RAG and self-reasoning to generate context-aware test oracles. Second, recognising that oracle generation may retrieve irrelevant constraints, misinterpret intent, or hallucinate requirements, we introduce a confidence-calibrated cascade judgment. This method quantifies verdict reliability via simulated expert agreement -- accepting high-confidence verdicts, escalating ambiguous cases, or abstaining when uncertain -- with empirical reliability guarantees backed by conformal risk control. An industrial case study on a production nutrition advisory application demonstrates that REAG achieves a 3.91/5 oracle quality score, reaching qualified or marginal oracle quality in 82% of cases. The confidence-calibrated cascade achieves 98.8% accuracy, improves oracle quality from 3.91 to 4.30 by filtering unqualified outputs, and delivers a 31.7% cost-efficiency improvement over single-judge baselines, validating industrial viability

</details>


### [117] [CAPRI: Contract-Aware Proof Repair for Isabelle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13459)
*Jim Woodcock,Gabriel Leite,Augusto Sampaio,Ran Wei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出CAPRI：将Isabelle证明修复与独立的、机器可读编辑契约检查结合，以确保LLM生成的修复不仅能通过Isabelle构建，还未越权修改受保护文本。实验显示，限制为仅编辑证明体可消除观测到的契约违规，且修复成功率仅略低于允许编辑完整理论的工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Isabelle接受提交的理论只能证明其形式化内容可被检查，不能保证LLM仅修改了开发者授权的范围。对于自动化证明修复，模型可能通过改动定义、定理陈述或其他受保护内容来绕过原始修复目标，因此需要可审计且可机械验证的修改边界。

Method: CAPRI在Isabelle验证候选证明之外，引入独立检查器校验候选仓库是否遵守编辑契约；同时保留提示词、模型提案、候选仓库、诊断信息、判定结果及哈希，以支持审计。评测覆盖4个开发中的12个失败证明，比较5种工作流；每个任务与条件重复3次，共180次运行，其中138次形成有效修复。研究还比较一次性与迭代式修复、仅证明体编辑与完整理论编辑，以及不同模型/接口配置。

Result: 在144个被Isabelle接受的终态候选中，有6个修改了受保护文本，且均来自允许编辑完整理论的迭代式工作流。仅证明体接口获得29/36个有效修复且零契约违规；对应的完整理论工作流为31/36。一次性修复为22/36，后续预先冻结的迭代工作流为32/36，但这些数值比较的是整体工作流，不能归因于单个机制。事后OpenRouter实验未改善指定的Luna对比。匹配示例的Sol配置为33/36，冻结的OpenAI Responses条件为29/36；单侧精确McNemar检验p=0.0625，差异未达到统计显著性。

Conclusion: CAPRI表明，证明修复系统应将“证明可通过”与“修改获授权”视为两个独立性质分别验证。通过收紧编辑接口至证明体，可在几乎不显著牺牲修复率的情况下避免观测到的越权修改。模型或配置间的表面成功率差异仍缺乏统计显著证据，后续研究需采用更大规模、预注册或冻结的评测来确认其稳健性。

Abstract: We address the use of large language models (LLMs) to help discover Isabelle proofs. An Isabelle build establishes that the submitted theory is accepted, but not that an LLM changed only what the developer authorised. We present CAPRI, a contract-aware repair workflow in which Isabelle checks the proof and an independent checker enforces a machine-readable edit contract. Prompts, proposals, candidate repositories, diagnostics, verdicts, and hashes are retained for audit. We evaluate five workflows on twelve failed proofs from four developments, with three replicates per task and condition, giving 180 runs and 138 valid repairs. Of 144 terminal candidates accepted by Isabelle, six had modified protected text; all arose in iterative workflows that could edit a complete theory. A proof-body-only interface produced 29/36 valid repairs and no contract violations, compared with 31/36 for the corresponding full-theory workflow. One-shot repair produced 22/36, while a later prospectively frozen iterative workflow produced 32/36; these figures compare complete workflows rather than individual mechanisms. A separate post hoc OpenRouter campaign found no improvement in the designated Luna comparisons. A Sol configuration with matched demonstrations produced 33/36 repairs, compared with 29/36 in the frozen OpenAI Responses condition, but the difference was not statistically significant in a one-sided exact McNemar test ($p=0.0625$).

</details>


### [118] [How Powerful are LLMs in Generating Formal Program Specifications?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13077)
*Fanpeng Yang,Xing Li,Shuling Wang,Jie An,Zeyu Sun,Shenghua Feng,Wenhan Wang,Weiyi Wang,Naijun Zhan,Fanjiang Xu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出 Coins：一个基于 Rocq 的程序规格生成评测框架。它不直接要求证明生成规格与实现一致或与参考规格语义等价，而是将候选规格实例化到可信测试用例上并生成具体证明义务，以更可靠地区分规格质量与证明难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化验证的瓶颈之一是精确规格的编写成本。虽然大语言模型已在定理证明和验证代码生成中展现能力，但其生成程序规格的真实水平缺乏可信评估。现有方法依赖实现符合性验证或规格等价证明，这些任务本身困难，容易将自动证明器的能力不足误判为候选规格质量较差。

Method: Coins 使用人工整理的 Rocq 规格和可信测试用例作为基础。对于待评估的候选规格，框架将其在测试输入下具体化，并构造可由 Rocq 检验的证明义务。其核心原则是形式推理的不对称性：证明成功可作为候选规格满足测试约束的强证据，而证明失败并不能直接说明规格错误。论文在 HumanEval 上开展大规模实验，比较不同生成规格在该评测机制下的表现。

Result: 实验表明，面向程序的形式规格生成仍然非常困难；同时，验证任务本身的复杂度会掩盖候选规格之间实际存在的质量差异。相比依赖全局语义等价或实现一致性的评测，基于测试实例的形式化推理能够提供更具区分度的信号。

Conclusion: 论文认为，理解大语言模型在规格综合中的能力，关键不只是扩大模型规模，更在于建立准确、可操作且能剥离证明难度干扰的评测方法。Coins 通过可信测试用例上的具体证明义务，为规格质量提供了更忠实的度量，但其结论主要反映候选规格在测试覆盖范围内的正确性，不能单独替代完整的语义正确性证明。

Abstract: Formal verification provides strong guarantees of software correctness, but its adoption is limited by the high cost of writing precise formal specifications. While recent large language models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in theorem proving and verified code generation, their true ability to generate program specifications remains unclear. Existing evaluations require either verifying implementation conformance or proving semantic equivalence between specifications, both of which are formidably difficult and may conflate proof difficulty with specification quality. To address this problem, we introduce Coins, a Rocq based evaluation framework that assesses specification quality by instantiating specifications under evaluation on trusted test cases and generating concrete proof obligations. This design aligns with the asymmetric nature of formal reasoning, where successful proofs provide reliable evidence while proof failures are inherently ambiguous. Using Coins, we conduct a large scale study on HumanEval with a curated set of human written Rocq specifications. Our results show that specification generation remains a formidable challenge, and that verification complexity can obscure genuine differences in specification quality. Overall, we find that accurate specification evaluation, rather than model scaling alone, is central to understanding the power of LLMs for specification synthesis, and that test case based formal reasoning offers a more faithful and discriminative measure of progress.

</details>


### [119] [Can Formal Specifications Be Synthesized from Tests Alone?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13240)
*Tianhai Liu,Maximilian Müller,Tobias Hey,Vitus Lüntzel,Muhammad Minhas,Anne Koziolek,Bernhard Beckert*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一种黑盒规格推断框架：仅利用测试代码与动态执行轨迹，让大语言模型生成候选 Java Modeling Language（JML）规格，并通过有界模型检查验证和迭代修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的规格生成通常需要读取源代码实现，因知识产权暴露风险及部署成本而难以在工业场景落地。该工作旨在在隐藏实现内部细节的条件下，降低形式化规格编写成本。

Method: 系统向大语言模型提供程序接口、选定测试输入及对应输出或状态变化，而不暴露实现代码；模型据此推断候选规格。随后在本地通过有界模型检查验证候选 JML 规格，并将检查器反馈用于下一轮生成与修正。

Result: 在 SpecGenBench 基准上的初步实验表明，测试信息能够引导模型生成具有实际语义的 JML 规格。实验同时发现，模型输出与检查器的兼容性，以及诊断反馈的质量和可利用性，是影响迭代修正可靠性的关键因素。

Conclusion: 测试与执行轨迹可作为源代码不可见条件下推断形式化规格的有效证据，为隐私和知识产权敏感环境中的规格自动化提供了可行方向。但要获得稳定可靠的结果，仍需改进规格语言约束、检查器兼容机制及面向模型的诊断反馈设计。

Abstract: Formal specifications offer strong guarantees, but remain costly to write manually. Recent LLM-based approaches automate this by inferring specifications from source code, yet their reliance on white-box access poses barriers to industrial adoption due to intellectual property risks and deployment costs. Our approach uses LLMs to infer candidate specifications solely from test code and dynamic execution traces: the LLM observes only the program interface, selected inputs, and corresponding outputs or state changes, while the implementation internals remain hidden. Candidate specifications are validated locally using bounded model checking, with feedback guiding iterative refinement. Initial results on the SpecGenBench benchmark suggest that tests can guide LLMs towards meaningful Java Modeling Language specifications, while also highlighting checker compatibility and diagnostic feedback as key challenges for reliable refinement.

</details>


### [120] [Refine After Generation: Toward Correct and Concise Patches in LLM-based Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13292)
*Wenqiang Luo,Jacky Keung,Xiaoyu Shi,Yicheng Sun,Boyang Yang,Zhou Yang,Haoye Tian*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文指出，现有基于大语言模型的自动程序修复系统即使能通过测试，其补丁通常仍显著大于且复杂于开发者补丁。作者提出生成后补丁精炼框架RECAP，在基本保持甚至提升问题解决数的同时，大幅降低补丁改动规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动程序修复评估主要关注测试通过与问题解决率，忽视了补丁的可审查性和维护成本。对SWE-bench Verified上28种方法的分析显示，成功补丁的中位总改动量、净改动量和圈复杂度分别比开发者补丁高121.78%、80.91%和43.99%，表明补丁冗长是普遍问题。

Method: 作者将补丁最小化从生成阶段解耦，定义为生成后的补丁精炼任务，并提出可插拔适配器RECAP。RECAP接收既有修复框架生成的补丁，由经监督微调和直接偏好优化训练的精炼器生成更小的等效修复；训练数据由多来源补丁对构成，并利用蒸馏后的推理轨迹辅助训练。论文还在4个宿主修复系统上，与提示词约束、提交拆分和最小化感知方法进行比较。

Result: 表层控制手段，如限定输出格式或要求最小改动，难以有效消除冗长性；其根源主要在迭代式精炼和大范围上下文等能力导向设计。基线方法虽然能缩小补丁，却会少解决49至217个实例。RECAP则将相对开发者补丁的平均总改动从+242.14%降至+4.24%，净改动从+348.24%降至-39.75%，并在不同宿主系统中保持或最多额外解决42个实例。

Conclusion: 补丁最小化并非简单的语法压缩，而需在保留语义正确性与修复能力的前提下移除不必要改动。将最小化作为独立的生成后精炼步骤，可比在生成阶段施加表面约束取得更优的规模—正确性权衡，并提升自动修复补丁的可审查性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have advanced automatic program repair (APR) to the point where agentic systems routinely resolve real-world, repository-level issues. Yet the generated patch has received little scrutiny beyond whether it passes tests.
  In this paper, we identify patch verbosity as a major yet overlooked concern in LLM-based APR. Characterizing 28 state-of-the-art approaches on SWE-bench Verified, we find that even successful patches are consistently larger and more complex than developer patches, with the median approach producing 121.78% more total changes, 80.91% more net changes, and 43.99% higher cyclomatic complexity. We further show that this verbosity is rooted in capability-oriented design choices such as iterative refinement and broad context, and can hardly be reduced by surface-level controls such as output format or minimality prompts. Motivated by these findings, we formulate post-generation patch refinement and propose RECAP, a lightweight, plug-and-play adapter that attaches to existing repair frameworks after generation. RECAP's refiner is trained via supervised fine-tuning and direct preference optimization with distilled reasoning traces, on a dataset of patch pairs we construct from multiple sources. Across four host systems, prompting, commit-untangling, and minimality-aware baselines reduce patch size only by sacrificing 49 to 217 resolved instances. In contrast, RECAP achieves a substantially better size-correctness tradeoff, cutting average total changes from +242.14% to +4.24% and net changes from +348.24% to -39.75% relative to developer patches while preserving or improving resolution by up to 42 instances. Our results indicate that minimality cannot be simply reduced to syntactic compression, and that decoupling minimization from generation offers a practical path to more reviewable repairs.

</details>


### [121] [Integration-First Structural Coverage for Embedded Software:Trace-Based Evidence, Hybrid Runtime Analysis, and Cross-Variant Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13322)
*Alexander Weiss,Albert Schulz,Michael Wittner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出面向嵌入式软件的集成优先覆盖率方案：以设备上的集成与系统测试为主要度量来源，结合基于嵌入式追踪的混合运行时分析，以及跨测试层级和构建变体的证据汇总，使优化版本中的分支、条件和MC/DC覆盖能够形成可审查的完整性证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统结构覆盖率通常在单元测试阶段采集，因为插桩和可观测性成本较低；但这与真实运行环境下的测试代表性不匹配。若直接在目标设备上进行经典插桩，会影响时序、内存占用和并发行为；仅依赖追踪日志重建覆盖率时，在高优化编译条件下又难以可靠恢复判定与条件边界。因此，需要一种既接近发布二进制行为、又能提供可信覆盖证据的方法。

Method: 方法包括三个部分：第一，定义集成优先的覆盖流程，将集成测试和系统测试作为基础测量，并通过显式闭环处理剩余缺口，将完整性表述为“已覆盖或已合理说明”。第二，采用嵌入式追踪作为观测通道，引入混合运行时分析（hRA）；该机制以最小化且保持语义的可观测性支架，使-O3优化构建中的判定与条件边界仍可在追踪流中区分，而覆盖状态维护和计数均放在目标设备之外。第三，利用Hyper Coverage汇总层整合不同测试层级、测试运行、软件变体和构建配置的覆盖证据。

Result: 该方案将目标代码到源代码的映射由启发式推断转变为可复核证据，并使发布近似二进制上的分支覆盖、条件覆盖和MC/DC度量具备可行性。汇总层可识别在所有相关变体中始终未被测试的源代码行，同时支持跨执行和配置的覆盖信息整合。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是把覆盖率采集从单元级、插桩主导的实践，扩展为以集成和系统级设备执行为中心的证据闭环。通过追踪、轻量语义保持观测支架与离线统计相结合，方案在降低对运行行为扰动的同时，提高了高优化嵌入式构建下结构覆盖证据的可信度和审查性。

Abstract: Structural coverage is widely used as evidence that testing is complete, yet in embedded projects it is predominantly collected at unit level, simply because that is where instrumentation and observability are inexpensive. This produces a mismatch. The most representative completeness signal would come from integration and system tests executed on the device under test, but classical instrumentation perturbs timing, memory footprint and concurrency behaviour, while purely trace-reconstructed coverage loses reliability for decisions and conditions as soon as the compiler optimizes aggressively. We address this mismatch from both ends. On the process side we describe an integrationfirst coverage strategy that treats integration and system tests as the baseline measurement and drives the residual gaps through an explicit closure loop, so that completeness is established as covered or justified rather than as covered alone. On the technical side we use embedded trace as the observation path and add hybrid runtime analysis (hRA): a minimal, semantics-preserving observability scaffolding that keeps decision and condition boundaries distinguishable in the trace stream of an optimized (-O3) build, while all coverage state and counting remain off-target. This converts object-to-source mapping from a heuristic reconstruction into reviewable evidence and makes branch, condition and MC/DC measurement practical on release-like binaries. Finally we describe Hyper Coverage, a consolidation layer that merges evidence across test levels, test runs, variants and build configurations, and that exposes source lines which remain untested in every relevant variant.

</details>


### [122] [Does Fixing Break Security? An Empirical Study of Security Degradation in Iterative LLM-Driven Infrastructure-as-Code Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13404)
*Benjamin Agyekum,Fabio Santos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文首次按逐轮迭代而非累计最优指标考察大语言模型修复基础设施即代码时的安全回归。严格口径下，约3.3%的场景出现真实回归；多数表面回归由多资源重构导致的测量伪象引起。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迭代式IaC修复研究通常报告单调不降的累计最优结果，掩盖了某次修复在解决既有问题时可能使原先通过的CIS检查失败的风险。作者旨在量化该风险、识别成因，并给出合理的迭代停止建议。

Method: 基于IaC-Eval的5,968条场景时间线，分析15种模型与检索增强配置下、最多5轮修复产生的4,440个相邻迭代转换。研究跟踪30个CIS检查ID，并通过代码差异归因。检测分别采用标准口径（包含相关表面失败）与严格口径（仅计入排他性的检查失败），同时比较代码改动量、检查波动性和后续自愈情况。

Result: 标准口径下，13.8%的场景和24.8%的迭代转换存在至少一次回归；严格口径下分别降至3.3%和5.2%，说明多数标准口径现象属于多资源测量伪象。资源重构占根因的79.0%。发生回归的转换具有2.6倍更高的代码改动量（Cohen's d=0.90）及4.9倍更高的严格口径检查波动性（d=1.49）。标准口径回归中36.6%会在平均1.2轮内自行恢复，第三轮是较优停止点。

Conclusion: 迭代式IaC修复确实可能引入安全回归，但采用保守且可辩护的严格定义后，真实风险约为场景级3.3%。系统应将安全回归检测纳入反馈闭环，并以第三轮作为兼顾修复收益与额外风险的实用迭代预算参考。

Abstract: Background: Iterative feedback loops are the dominant paradigm for improving LLM-generated Infrastructure-as-Code (IaC): validators such as Checkov and terraform validate feed error signals back for successive repair attempts. Prior work reports cumulative-best metrics, which are non-decreasing by construction, so the raw per-iteration security trajectory has never been examined for IaC. Aims: We study security regression (a previously-passing CIS Benchmark check that fails after a repair iteration) to determine whether and how often iterative LLM repair degrades security while fixing other issues. Method: We analyze 5,968 scenario timelines from the IaC-Eval benchmark, each one scenario run through one configuration for up to 5 repair iterations. The 15 configurations (six model-specific RAG, nine model-aggregated non-RAG, three temperatures each) yield 4,440 iteration transitions with Checkov data on both sides. We track 30 individual CIS check IDs and classify root causes from code diffs, under two detection modes: standard (inclusive) and strict (exclusive check failures only). Results: Under standard detection, 13.8% of scenarios (24.8% of transitions) exhibit at least one regression. Under strict detection the rate falls to 3.3% of scenarios (5.2% of transitions), indicating most apparent regressions are multi-resource measurement artifacts. Resource restructuring (79.0%) is the dominant root cause. Regression transitions show 2.6x more code churn (Cohen's d=0.90) and 4.9x higher strict-mode check volatility (d=1.49). Of standard-mode regressions, 36.6% self-correct within an average of 1.2 iterations; iteration 3 is the optimal stopping point. Conclusions: Iterative IaC repair does introduce security regressions, but the conservative, defensible rate is about 3.3% of scenarios. Our findings motivate security-aware feedback-loop design and actionable iteration-budget guidance.

</details>


### [123] [LLM-Assisted Dynamic Threat Analysis for Attacker-Reachable Software Weaknesses in Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13450)
*Md Wasiul Haque,Sagar Dasgupta,Mizanur Rahman,Md Rayhanur Rahman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文评估大语言模型在Autoware自动驾驶软件栈中自动生成动态验证工件的能力。结论是：主要瓶颈并非静态候选点发现或模糊测试，而是将生成的测试桩、依赖与真实构建系统可靠集成；在给定预算内，未动态确认任何候选缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶软件具有大规模、强安全约束和复杂依赖等特征。静态分析虽可发现潜在输入到安全输出的可达路径，但要判断其是否实际可触发，需要可编译、可执行并可用于模糊测试的验证工件。人工构造此类工件成本高，论文因此考察LLM能否实现自动化。

Method: 研究对185个软件包进行编译器精确的静态分析，识别1,375条决策规则、2,274项验证检查及482条输入至安全输出的数据流；据此建立缺陷分类并抽样740个可达点。作者比较两种本地开源权重大模型、一个不提供静态上下文的消融设置和一个朴素模板基线，共生成3,700组工件。工件在真实构建环境中经由编译器反馈迭代修复，并在可执行时于Sanitizer辅助下进行模糊测试。

Result: 80%的首次编译失败由依赖连接、构建配置及接口集成问题导致，而非生成代码本身的程序逻辑错误。推理型模型的测试桩首次编译成功率为64%，显著高于代码专用模型的6%。推理型模型虽可通过大量桩代码修复达到对象文件可编译，但不足半数测试桩最终能进入模糊测试阶段。记录到的37次崩溃均发生于人工或模型生成的桩代码，而非Autoware原始代码；在实验预算内，没有任何静态候选点获得动态可利用性确认。

Conclusion: LLM能够在一定程度上辅助生成动态分析工件，但其在大型真实软件栈上的有效性受制于构建系统、跨包依赖、类型接口和运行时环境的集成复杂度。后续工作应优先改进依赖解析、真实组件复用、最小化桩代码及端到端可执行性验证，而不能仅关注候选缺陷生成能力或模糊测试策略。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles depend on large safety-critical software stacks, where weaknesses reachable from adversarial inputs may affect steering, braking, or other control decisions. Static analysis can identify candidate sites, but dynamically confirming exploitability requires executable test artifacts that are difficult to construct manually. We investigate whether large language models (LLMs) can automate this process for Autoware, an open-source autonomous-driving stack. We perform compiler-precise static analysis across 185 packages, identifying 1,375 decision rules, 2,274 validation checks, and 482 input-to-safety-output flows, from which we derive a weakness taxonomy and sample 740 reachable sites. Two local open-weight LLMs, a no-static-context ablation, and a naive-template baseline generate 3,700 artifact sets, which are compiled against the real build under sanitizers, repaired through compiler-in-the-loop feedback, and fuzzed when executable. The main result is a build-integration failure taxonomy showing that 80% of first-shot compilation failures arise from dependency wiring rather than program logic. The reasoning model compiled 64% of harnesses on the first attempt, compared with 6% for the code-specialized model. Repair achieved full object-compileability for the reasoning model only through extensive stubbing; fewer than half of its harnesses reached the fuzzer, and all 37 observed crashes originated in stubbed code rather than Autoware. No candidate weakness was dynamically confirmed within budget. These results show that build integration, not candidate generation or fuzzing, is the primary barrier to reliable LLM-assisted dynamic analysis of full autonomous-vehicle software stacks.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Synchronous Observers Revisited for Runtime Verification of Lustre Using STL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12693)
*Logan Kenwright,Partha Roop,Sobhan Chatterjee,Nathan Allen*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出将同步时序逻辑（SSTL）自动编译为Lustre同步观察器的方法，使同一性质既可用于在线运行时监控，也可接入Kind2进行静态验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STL常用于运行时验证，而同步语言通常以可组合的观察器表达安全性质。已有STL到同步观察器的工作对有界算子的嵌套能力有限，且较少同时支持在线监控与静态模型检查；因此需要一种更通用、模块化的编译方案。

Method: 作者针对STL的同步片段设计到Lustre数据流程序的模块化编译技术。该方法支持任意层次嵌套的有界性质，并允许最外层采用全局无界算子以实现持续在线监控。生成的观察器可与待验证程序组合执行；此外，论文实现交互式可视化工具，在可编辑轨迹上展示性质的三值判定结果。

Result: 所生成的Lustre观察器同时具备可执行规格、运行时监控器和静态验证辅助模型三种作用，并可由Kind2模型检查器进行分析。作者在弹簧—质量系统及跟驰巡航控制器两个文献案例上进行了评估，表明方法能够处理所述监控与验证任务。

Conclusion: 该工作统一了SSTL性质的在线监控和静态验证流程。其主要贡献在于支持任意有界嵌套的模块化编译、支持无界外层在线判定，以及提供三值语义的交互式轨迹可视化，从而提升同步时序性质规格的可复用性与实用性。

Abstract: Signal Temporal Logic (STL) is a popular formalism for the temporal safety properties of cyber-physical systems, most often used for runtime verification. In the synchronous family of languages, safety properties are instead expressed as synchronous observers, modules composed with a program for static verification, which are also runnable specifications suitable for runtime verification, though this use is rarely explored. We present a technique for compiling the synchronous fragment of STL (SSTL) into synchronous observers in the dataflow language Lustre. Unlike previous work, we allow arbitrary nesting of bounded SSTL properties via modular compilation, and admit a globally unbounded outer operator for online monitoring; the resulting observers serve both runtime verification and, as a by-product, static verification with the Kind2 model checker. We further contribute an interactive visualiser that renders a property's three-valued verdict over an editable trace, and evaluate on two case studies from the literature: a spring-mass system and a car-following cruise controller.

</details>


### [125] [Computing Fixed Points using Dependency Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13020)
*Giorgio Bacci,Giovanni Bacci,Kim G. Larsen,Daniele Toller*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出用于求解带底元诺特偏序集上方程组的全局与局部算法，通过依赖预言机按需探索变量依赖，以正确且可配置地完成局部不动点计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多验证问题可归约为偏序结构上的方程组求解，但传统方法通常需要广泛甚至完整地遍历系统。作者旨在仅计算目标变量所必需的部分，从而降低求解开销，同时保留对不同应用场景的适应性。

Method: 方法以变量依赖预言机为核心：预言机指导方程系统的按需探索，并为局部不动点计算提供可靠的终止判据。该框架支持定制、组合及对预言机进行过近似，使使用者可在不损害正确性的前提下，以精度换取性能。

Result: 作者将原型实现与既有文献中的算法进行比较。实验表明，该实现具有竞争力，并且在不少场景中优于专用求解方案；同时其实现保持相对简洁，能够适配多类应用领域。

Conclusion: 依赖预言机为偏序方程组的局部求解提供了统一、正确且灵活的机制。该方法的主要价值在于按需计算与可控精度—性能权衡，兼具通用性和实用性能。

Abstract: We present global and local algorithms for solving systems of equations over Noetherian posets with a bottom element, a general setting underlying many verification problems. Our algorithms compute the solution of a selected variable by restricting exploration to those parts of the system required to determine its value. We achieve this by computing variable dependencies by means of dependency oracles. Oracles guide the exploration of the system and provide sound termination criteria for local fixed-point computation. A key advantage of our approach is its flexibility: oracles can be customized, composed, or over-approximated, offering a principled way to trade precision for performance without compromising correctness. We evaluate our solution against existing algorithms from the literature and show that our prototype implementation is competitive and often outperforms specialized solutions, while remaining simple and adaptable across diverse application domains.

</details>


### [126] [Completeness and incompleteness of basic matching logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13306)
*Xiaohong Chen,Grigore Rosu*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文系统刻画了基础匹配逻辑的完备性边界：任意单排序有限元签名下可获得全局完备性；加入最小不动点后有效公理化失效；多排序情形中全局完备性一般失败，且三个排序已构成反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础匹配逻辑不含定义性算子，符号解释为集合值运算，元素变量表示单元素集合并由存在量词绑定。研究旨在解决其在任意、包括无限假设集上的语义蕴涵与形式可证性是否一致，并明确不动点、排序数及名词对该性质的影响。

Method: 作者将全局假设集Γ局部化为理论Δ_Γ，证明Γ对公式φ的语义蕴涵等价于Δ_Γ下的局部语义蕴涵，并进一步等价于Γ对φ的可证性。核心语义等价通过双覆盖构造建立。负面部分采用特定签名上的不可递归枚举性论证、三排序可满足理论反例，以及对满足局部化约束的良基演算类别的阻碍分析。

Result: 对于任意单排序有限元签名且无不动点的基础匹配逻辑，Γ⊨φ当且仅当Γ⊢φ，即使Γ无限亦成立；由此定义性扩展是保守的。含一个一元和两个二元符号、无常量的签名下，加入最小不动点时有效性不可递归枚举，因此不存在证明关系可递归枚举且哪怕弱完备的可靠演算。三排序时，对某个可满足Γ全局完备性失败。对应地，任意元模态上的状态变量量化语言在无名词时全局完备，而加入名词后，在所述良基且局部化保持的演算类中不可能全局完备。

Conclusion: 论文给出清晰二分：单排序、无不动点的基础匹配逻辑具有全局完备性；不动点破坏有效可公理化，多排序则破坏一般全局完备性。因而原有完备性猜想在单排序成立、在一般多排序框架中不成立；结果同时精确界定了定义性扩展与混合逻辑名词机制的证明论限制。

Abstract: Basic matching logic is matching logic without definedness. Symbols are interpreted as set-valued operations, element variables denote singletons and are bound by $\exists$, and no connective uniformly internalizes totality. For basic matching logic without fixpoints over an arbitrary one-sorted finitary signature, we prove \emph{global completeness} ($Γ\vDash\varphi$ iff $Γ\vdash\varphi$, for arbitrary, possibly infinite $Γ$) and, as a corollary, conservativity of the definedness extension. The proof localizes $Γ$ to a theory $Δ_Γ$ and reduces semantic consequence and derivability to the same local relation: $Γ\vDash\varphi$ iff $Δ_Γ\vDash_\text{loc}\varphi$ iff $Γ\vdash\varphi$. A double-cover construction establishes the semantic equivalence. Least fixpoints destroy effective axiomatizability. Over a signature with one unary and two binary symbols and no constants, validity is not recursively enumerable; hence no sound calculus with a recursively enumerable proof relation is even weakly complete, already for the empty theory and without definedness. The positive result is also sharp in the number of sorts. Global completeness fails with three sorts for a satisfiable $Γ$. Thus the completeness conjecture holds for one sort and fails in general. The negative results arise from sort flow, fixpoint effectivity, and, for hybrid logic, an obstruction to every well-founded calculus whose leaves are hypotheses or valid patterns and whose rules respect localization. This yields a matching-logic-independent dichotomy: the language with state variables bound by $\exists$ and $\forall$ over modalities of arbitrary arity is globally complete without nominals, while no calculus in that well-founded class is globally complete once nominals are added.

</details>


### [127] [A Dense Weisfeiler-Leman Algorithm for Deciding Bounded-Cliquewidth Homomorphism Indistinguishability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13382)
*Radu Curticapean,Daniel Neuen,Amir Nikabadi,Tim Seppelt,Ben Young*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文研究以某一图类为测试集合的同态不可区分性：两个图若从该类中每个图到它们的同态数相同，则视为等价。作者首次为稠密图类给出一般可判定性结果，核心是用于团宽至多k图类的“稠密Weisfeiler-Leman”算法，并将结果推广到有界团宽的CMSO_1可定义图类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同态不可区分性统一刻画了多种图同构松弛，但测试图类通常无限，因而判定两个输入图是否不可区分并不显然可计算。已有可判定结果主要针对稀疏图类；团宽有界图类是树宽有界图类在稠密情形下的重要对应物。论文旨在填补稠密图类上缺乏通用判定算法的空白。

Method: 作者提出稠密Weisfeiler-Leman算法，用于判定相对于团宽至多k的测试图类的同态不可区分性。其关键区别于经典k维Weisfeiler-Leman算法：经典方法给顶点k元组着色，而该方法对顶点子集的k元组着色。进一步，论文建立元定理，将可判定性扩展至任意同时满足“CMSO_1可定义”和“团宽有界”的图类；并针对有界线性团宽类改进复杂度分析。

Result: 对于团宽至多k的图类，稠密Weisfeiler-Leman算法可决定相应的同态不可区分性。对于任意CMSO_1可定义的有界团宽图类，该问题可在随机化指数时间内判定。若图类具有有界线性团宽，则问题属于PSPACE；作者还构造了一个此类图类，使问题达到PSPACE完全性，从而证明该复杂度上界是紧的。

Conclusion: 论文给出了首批适用于稠密图类的同态不可区分性通用算法与复杂度刻画。其主要贡献是以团宽为结构参数建立稠密版本的Weisfeiler-Leman框架，并通过逻辑可定义性元定理显著扩展适用范围；PSPACE完全性结果表明，在线性团宽有界场景中所得复杂度界限无法在一般情况下进一步降低。

Abstract: Two graphs $G$ and $H$ are homomorphism indistinguishable over a graph class $\mathcal{F}$ if they admit the same number of homomorphisms from every graph in $\mathcal{F}$. A wide range of relaxations of graph isomorphism arise this way: isomorphism itself over the class of all graphs [Lovász, Acta Math. Hung. 1967], equivalence under the $k$-dimensional Weisfeiler-Leman algorithm over the graphs of treewidth $\leq k$ [Dvořák, J. Graph Theory 2010], and quantum isomorphism over planar graphs [Mančinska-Roberson, FOCS 2020]. Since the class $\mathcal{F}$ is typically infinite, it is not clear a priori whether homomorphism indistinguishability over $\mathcal{F}$ is decidable; for planar graphs it is undecidable. Every class for which decidability was previously known is sparse.
  We give the first decidability results for dense graph classes: We introduce the dense Weisfeiler-Leman algorithm that decides homomorphism indistinguishability over the class of graphs of cliquewidth $\leq k$, the dense counterpart of treewidth. This relation was not previously known to be decidable. The algorithm colors $k$-tuples of vertex subsets rather than $k$-tuples of vertices.
  Beyond the class of all graphs of cliquewidth $\leq k$, we prove a general meta-theorem: homomorphism indistinguishability over every $\mathsf{CMSO}_1$-definable graph class of bounded cliquewidth is decidable, in randomized exponential time. For classes of bounded linear cliquewidth the bound improves to $\mathsf{PSPACE}$, and we show this is tight by exhibiting such a class for which the problem is $\mathsf{PSPACE}$-complete. These are the first general algorithms for homomorphism indistinguishability over dense graph classes.

</details>


### [128] [Runtime Monitoring of Distributed Cyber-Physical Systems Without a Global Clock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13486)
*Charles Koll,Houssam Abbas*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文首次针对时钟存在漂移、缺乏统一全局时间的分布式信息物理系统，给出连续时间时序逻辑监测的理论刻画与算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式信息物理系统中，各节点使用彼此漂移的本地时钟，使得稠密时间时序约束的语义及公式满足性难以定义；现有方法多面向离散时间，或要求将全局时间约束预先显式映射到本地时钟，因此难以直接适用于控制工程场景。

Method: 作者面向仍包含全部时序算子的信号时序逻辑子片段，扩展了满足性信号的定义以适配部分同步时钟模型，并利用多维部分同步时间的几何结构设计监测算法。算法输入分布式输出信号及逻辑公式，计算可能满足该公式的全部全局时刻集合；同时推导最坏情形复杂度，并实现了该算法的保守近似版本。

Result: 该方法能够在时钟漂移条件下对分布式系统实施稠密时间逻辑监测，并输出所有可能满足规格的全局时刻。实验表明，所实现的可靠近似算法具有实际可用性，能够处理最高约50个智能体的场景。

Conclusion: 论文弥补了分布式、部分同步信息物理系统在连续时间形式化监测方面的关键空白。其核心贡献是将全局时间语义的不确定性转化为可计算的可能满足时刻集合，为分布式混合控制系统的调试与验证提供了理论基础和可扩展的工程路径。

Abstract: We give the first theoretical characterization, and the first algorithm, for continuous monitoring of a distributed Cyber-Physical System (CPS) against a dense-time temporal logic specification. A distributed CPS is composed of multiple agents, each with a local clock; these clocks drift from each other, so there is no well-defined global time. When monitoring such a system's output signal against a temporal logic specification, it is not evident how to interpret the temporal constraints of the formula, and what satisfaction means. Yet CPS designers, like control engineers, typically think of their system's operation in terms of global time. Most existing techniques for monitoring distributed systems work with discrete-time specifications not suitable for CPS, and/or require an explicit mapping of temporal constraints to local clocks. We introduce an algorithm that addresses the above challenges for a fragment of Signal Temporal Logic (STL) that still includes all temporal operators. It relies on a novel extension of satisfaction signals to this partially synchronous setting (where clocks drift), and an analysis of the geometry of multi-dimensional partially synchronous time. The algorithm returns the set of all possible global moments that can satisfy the specification. Knowledge of these possible global moments is important for debugging distributed hybrid control systems such as fleets of drones and electrical grids. We derive the worst-case complexity of the algorithm, and implement a sound approximation of it that experimentally illustrates effective monitoring, even in scenarios of up to 50 agents.

</details>
